  904:     # classify differently across these categories still renders
  905:     # differently, and that limit is stated rather than papered over.
  906:     #
  907:     # THE BOUND IS ON THE OUTPUT. Truncating the INPUT to 200 and then
  908:     # expanding escapes produced 1,211 characters for a control-heavy
  909:     # name, so the advertised bound has to be enforced while appending.
  910:     $s = [string]$name
  911:     $limit = 200
  912:     $marker = "[truncated]"
  913:     $room = $limit - $marker.Length
  914:     $escaped = @([System.Globalization.UnicodeCategory]::Control,
  915:                  [System.Globalization.UnicodeCategory]::Format,
  916:                  [System.Globalization.UnicodeCategory]::LineSeparator,
  917:                  [System.Globalization.UnicodeCategory]::ParagraphSeparator,
  918:                  [System.Globalization.UnicodeCategory]::Surrogate,
  919:                  [System.Globalization.UnicodeCategory]::PrivateUse,
  920:                  [System.Globalization.UnicodeCategory]::OtherNotAssigned)
  921:     # NON-BMP CHARACTERS ARE ALL ESCAPED, which the category list above
  922:     # does not make obvious. This walks UTF-16 code UNITS, so a
  923:     # supplementary character arrives as two units both classified
  924:     # Surrogate, and the escape set therefore covers every codepoint in
  925:     # planes 1 through 16. An emoji or a CJK Extension B name renders
  926:     # escaped. That is intended, not an oversight to be repaired.
  927:     $sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
  928:     foreach ($ch in $s.ToCharArray()) {
  929:         $cat = [System.Globalization.CharUnicodeInfo]::GetUnicodeCategory($ch)
  930:         if ($escaped -contains $cat) {
  931:             $unit = "\u" + ([int]$ch).ToString("x4")
  932:         } else {
  933:             $unit = [string]$ch
  934:         }
  935:         if (($sb.Length + $unit.Length) -gt $room) {
  936:             [void]$sb.Append($marker)
  937:             break
  938:         }
  939:         [void]$sb.Append($unit)
  940:     }
  941:     return $sb.ToString()
  942: }
  943: 
  944: function Write-SourceDriftExplanation($mirrorPath, $liveManifest) {
  945:     # Runs ONLY after the source-status refusal below has been printed.
  946:     #
  947:     # THE WHOLE BODY IS WRAPPED. What protects the verdict is not any
  948:     # property of this function - Write-Output emits into the pipeline
  949:     # like any other command, and a caller COULD capture it - but the
  950:     # fact that its one caller ignores the output and reaches `exit 1`
  951:     # unconditionally. The wrap is here so that a fault in explaining a
  952:     # refusal cannot replace that refusal with an error.
  953:     try {
  954:         $smResult = Get-SourceManifestSidecarPath $mirrorPath
  955:         if ($smResult.Kind -eq "root") {
  956:             Write-Output ("  what moved: unknown - the mirror path is a" +
  957:                 " filesystem root, so no advisory manifest can sit beside it")
  958:             return
  959:         }
  960:         if ($smResult.Kind -ne "ok") {
  961:             Write-Output ("  what moved: unknown - the mirror path could not" +
  962:                 " be resolved (" + (Format-AdvisoryName $smResult.Reason) + ")")
  963:             return
  964:         }
  965:         $sidecar = $smResult.Path
  966:         $shown = Format-AdvisoryName $sidecar
  967:         # ONE HANDLE measures and reads. A separate Get-Item followed by
  968:         # a separate ReadAllBytes bounds nothing: the file can grow or be
  969:         # replaced between them, and Get-Item's failure is NON-TERMINATING
  970:         # in this script, which never sets $ErrorActionPreference, so an
  971:         # unreadable file left the size test unmade and carried on.
  972:         $limit = 67108864
  973:         $bytes = $null
  974:         $fs = [System.IO.File]::Open($sidecar, 'Open', 'Read', 'Read')
  975:         try {
  976:             if ($fs.Length -gt $limit) {
  977:                 Write-Output ("  what moved: unknown - the advisory manifest" +
  978:                     " at " + $shown + " is " + $fs.Length + " bytes, past" +
  979:                     " this reader's limit")
  980:                 return
  981:             }
  982:             $bytes = New-Object byte[] ([int]$fs.Length)
  983:             $off = 0
  984:             while ($off -lt $bytes.Length) {
  985:                 $n = $fs.Read($bytes, $off, $bytes.Length - $off)
  986:                 if ($n -le 0) { break }
  987:                 $off += $n
  988:             }
  989:         } finally { $fs.Dispose() }
  990:         # DECODE THE BYTES EXPLICITLY. A StreamReader detects a byte-order
  991:         # mark and consumes it, so a first pathname that legitimately
  992:         # begins with U+FEFF would lose that character silently and read
  993:         # as a different path.
  994:         $text = (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false, $true)).GetString($bytes)
  995:         $read = Read-BoundedRecords $text 200000
  996:         $drift = Get-ManifestDrift $read.Records $liveManifest
  997:         Write-Output ("  the lines below come from an UNAUTHENTICATED file" +
  998:             " beside the mirror (" + $shown + ") and are advisory only")
  999:         if ($read.Truncated) {
 1000:             Write-Output ("  note: the advisory manifest holds more than" +
 1001:                 " 200000 records; the remainder was NOT examined, so this" +
 1002:                 " explanation is incomplete")
 1003:         }
 1004:         if ($drift.Malformed -gt 0) {
 1005:             Write-Output ("  note: " + $drift.Malformed + " advisory record(s)" +
 1006:                 " could not be read, so this explanation is incomplete")
 1007:         }
 1008:         $groups = @(@("content changed", $drift.Changed),
 1009:                     @("entered manifest coverage", $drift.Entered),
 1010:                     @("left manifest coverage", $drift.Left))
 1011:         $any = $false
 1012:         foreach ($g in $groups) {
 1013:             $names = @($g[1])
 1014:             if ($names.Count -eq 0) { continue }
 1015:             $any = $true
 1016:             Write-Output ("  " + $g[0] + " (" + $names.Count + "):")
 1017:             $printed = 0
 1018:             foreach ($n in $names) {
 1019:                 if ($printed -ge 20) {
 1020:                     Write-Output ("    ... and " + ($names.Count - 20) + " more")
 1021:                     break
 1022:                 }
 1023:                 Write-Output ("    " + (Format-AdvisoryName $n))
 1024:                 $printed++
 1025:             }
 1026:         }
 1027:         if (-not $any) {
 1028:             Write-Output ("  what moved: the advisory manifest did not" +
 1029:                 " identify the cause. It records no content difference," +
 1030:                 " which is also what a stale or replaced manifest records.")
 1031:         }
 1032:     } catch {
 1033:         Write-Output ("  what moved: unknown - the advisory explanation" +
 1034:             " failed (" + (Format-AdvisoryName $_.Exception.Message) + ")")
 1035:     }
 1036: }
 1037: 
 1038: $toplevel = $true
 1039: 
 1040: # THE THIRD TEST SEAM, declared here rather than beside the other two
 1041: # because it is read inside Get-FilesBeneath, which runs during the
 1042: # FIRST Get-StatusSha256 call - the source status taken before the copy,
 1043: # and the verify mode's captures, both of which precede that block. Same
 1044: # one-way rule: a BOOLEAN that only ADDS a failure inside the link
 1045: # expansion, never supplies a value and never suppresses a measurement.
 1046: $seamFailLinkTarget = [bool]$env:PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_LINK_TARGET
 1047: 
 1048: if (-not (Test-Path $RepoRoot)) {
 1049:     Write-Output "ERROR: $RepoRoot does not exist"
 1050:     exit 2
 1051: }
 1052: $RepoRoot = (Resolve-Path $RepoRoot).Path
 1053: 
 1054: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 1055: # VERIFY MODE - step 6 of the construction bridge, run immediately before
 1056: # every fresh and resumed dispatch.
 1057: #
 1058: # WHAT IT PROVES: the two-HEAD gate proves committed-HEAD freshness.
 1059: # Non-HEAD inputs are bound in the constructed mirror's manifest AT
 1060: # CONSTRUCTION TIME, and changes after construction are caught by TWO
 1061: # status-based fingerprints, one per side. The source-status comparison
 1062: # catches source-side drift WHEN IT IS VISIBLE TO IT: changes that move
 1063: # the status listing, or that alter the content of a path the listing
 1064: # names. The mirror-state comparison catches the same class of change
 1065: # made directly INSIDE the mirror after construction - an uncommitted
 1066: # edit to a file the mirror's own HEAD still calls clean, or a file
 1067: # added to the mirror - which the two-HEAD gate cannot see because
 1068: # neither head moves. A tracked file git reports CLEAN, on either side,
 1069: # is covered by neither fingerprint, so a raw-byte change surviving the
 1070: # clean filter unchanged is NOT covered. The contract region carries the
 1071: # same qualification; this comment said "are detected" flat until round
 1072: # 3 of the mode-diff debate pointed out that the code and the contract
 1073: # had drifted apart again, in the direction of the code claiming more.
 1074: #
 1075: # Two further refusals guard the comparison itself, both ahead of any
 1076: # digest work: the source and the mirror must not be the same directory
 1077: # - otherwise every remaining comparison is trivially satisfied whenever
 1078: # the two heads already agree, which they do whenever the mirror needed
 1079: # no remediation commit - and the live -MirrorPath must canonically
 1080: # match -ExpectedMirrorPath. Both are CALLER ARGUMENTS in the SAME
 1081: # invocation rather than values read back from the construction record,
 1082: # so the guarantee is narrow: it catches a verify whose two path
 1083: # arguments disagree, and it does NOT refuse a mirror rebuilt somewhere
 1084: # else with both arguments updated to the new path.
 1085: #
 1086: # Neither status comparison is decoration. An edit to an untracked or
 1087: # ignored review input moves NEITHER head, and that content class is
 1088: # precisely what the mirror exists to carry, so a gate built only on
 1089: # HEADs would be blind in the middle of the feature.
 1090: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 1091: if ($VerifyIdentity) {
 1092:     # Resolve THROUGH THE PROVIDER here too. Build does this below and
 1093:     # verify did not, so a relative -MirrorPath resolved against the
 1094:     # process location for Test-Path and against the provider location
 1095:     # for git - the same divergence this file's own history block calls
 1096:     # an ordinary PowerShell condition. Verify only reads, so the
 1097:     # realistic failure was a spurious block rather than a wrong pass,
 1098:     # but a tool should not teach one lesson in one mode and forget it
 1099:     # in the other.
 1100:     $MirrorPath = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($MirrorPath)
 1101:     $ExpectedMirrorPath = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($ExpectedMirrorPath)
 1102:     $shaRx = '^[0-9a-f]{40}$'
 1103:     if ($SourceHead -cnotmatch $shaRx) {
 1104:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded source head is missing or not a" +
 1105:             " commit id, so nothing was compared")
 1106:         exit 1
 1107:     }
 1108:     if ($MirrorHead -cnotmatch $shaRx) {
 1109:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded mirror head is missing or not a" +
 1110:             " commit id, so nothing was compared")
 1111:         exit 1
 1112:     }
 1113:     if ($SourceStatusSha256 -cnotmatch '^[0-9a-f]{64}$') {
 1114:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded source status hash is missing or" +
 1115:             " malformed, so nothing was compared")
 1116:         exit 1
 1117:     }
 1118:     if ($MirrorStateSha256 -cnotmatch '^[0-9a-f]{64}$') {
 1119:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded mirror state hash is missing or" +
 1120:             " malformed, so nothing was compared")
 1121:         exit 1
 1122:     }
 1123: 
 1124:     # BEFORE ANY DIGEST WORK. A source pointed at itself as the mirror
 1125:     # would satisfy every remaining comparison trivially whenever the
 1126:     # two heads already agree, which they do whenever the mirror needed
 1127:     # no remediation commit.
 1128:     $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
 1129:     $rrNorm = $RepoRoot.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
 1130:     $mpNorm = $MirrorPath.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
 1131:     if ($mpNorm.Equals($rrNorm, $cmp)) {
 1132:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source and the mirror are the same" +
 1133:             " directory ($MirrorPath), so nothing was compared")
 1134:         exit 1
 1135:     }
 1136:     # The live -MirrorPath must canonically match -ExpectedMirrorPath.
 1137:     # Both are caller arguments in this same invocation, so this catches
 1138:     # a call whose two path arguments disagree. It does NOT refuse a
 1139:     # mirror rebuilt elsewhere with both arguments updated.
 1140:     $expNorm = $ExpectedMirrorPath.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
 1141:     if (-not $mpNorm.Equals($expNorm, $cmp)) {
 1142:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: -MirrorPath ($MirrorPath) does not match" +
 1143:             " -ExpectedMirrorPath ($ExpectedMirrorPath) in this same call")
 1144:         exit 1
 1145:     }
 1146: 
 1147:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $MirrorPath -PathType Container)) {
 1148:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror at $MirrorPath is gone, so its" +
 1149:             " identity could not be measured")
 1150:         exit 1
 1151:     }
 1152: 
 1153:     $liveSource = Get-HeadSha $RepoRoot
 1154:     if (-not $liveSource.Ok) {
 1155:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $liveSource.Reason + " - an unread source" +
 1156:             " head is never a matching one")
 1157:         exit 1
 1158:     }
 1159:     if ($liveSource.Sha -ne $SourceHead) {
 1160:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source head moved since construction" +
 1161:             " (recorded " + $SourceHead + ", live " + $liveSource.Sha +
 1162:             ") - rebuild the mirror")
 1163:         exit 1
 1164:     }
 1165: 
 1166:     $liveMirror = Get-HeadSha $MirrorPath
 1167:     if (-not $liveMirror.Ok) {
 1168:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $liveMirror.Reason + " - an unread mirror" +
 1169:             " head is never a matching one")
 1170:         exit 1
 1171:     }
 1172:     if ($liveMirror.Sha -ne $MirrorHead) {
 1173:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror head changed since construction" +
 1174:             " (recorded " + $MirrorHead + ", live " + $liveMirror.Sha +
 1175:             ") - something wrote into the mirror")
 1176:         exit 1
 1177:     }
 1178: 
 1179:     $liveStatus = Get-StatusSha256 $RepoRoot
 1180:     if (-not $liveStatus.Ok) {
 1181:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status could not be captured (" +
 1182:             $liveStatus.Reason + ") - an unmade measurement is never a clean" +
 1183:             " one")
 1184:         exit 1
 1185:     }
 1186:     if ($liveStatus.Sha -ne $SourceStatusSha256) {
 1187:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status changed since construction" +
 1188:             " - a tracked, untracked or ignored review input moved without" +
 1189:             " moving either head, so the mirror no longer carries what the" +
 1190:             " source holds. Rebuild the mirror.")
 1191:         Write-SourceDriftExplanation $MirrorPath $liveStatus.Manifest
 1192:         exit 1
 1193:     }
 1194: 
 1195:     # THE MIRROR-SIDE FINGERPRINT, recomputed live. Catches an
 1196:     # uncommitted edit to a file the mirror's own HEAD still calls clean,
 1197:     # or a file added to the mirror after construction - the case the
 1198:     # two-HEAD gate cannot see because neither head moves for it.
 1199:     $liveMirrorState = Get-StatusSha256 $MirrorPath
 1200:     if (-not $liveMirrorState.Ok) {
 1201:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror's current state could not be" +
 1202:             " measured (" + $liveMirrorState.Reason + ") - an unmade" +
 1203:             " measurement is never a clean one")
 1204:         exit 1
 1205:     }
 1206:     if ($liveMirrorState.Sha -ne $MirrorStateSha256) {
 1207:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror's contents changed since" +
 1208:             " construction - something edited or added a file inside the" +
 1209:             " mirror without moving its head. Rebuild the mirror.")
 1210:         exit 1
 1211:     }
 1212: 
 1213:     Write-Output "identity: verified"
 1214:     exit 0
 1215: }
 1216: 
 1217: # RESOLVE ONCE, THROUGH THE PROVIDER, BEFORE ANY GUARD. An earlier
 1218: # revision guarded `[IO.Path]::GetFullPath($MirrorPath)`, which resolves a
 1219: # relative path against the PROCESS working directory, while `Test-Path`
 1220: # and `Remove-Item -Recurse -Force` further down resolve the same parameter
 1221: # against PowerShell's PROVIDER location. When a caller's two locations
 1222: # differ - any in-session use after Set-Location - the guard approved one
 1223: # absolute target and the recursive delete destroyed another. Found by the
 1224: # mode-diff review, 2026-07-28; a prior review had called it theoretical,
 1225: # which was wrong: -MirrorPath is a public parameter and the divergence is
 1226: # an ordinary PowerShell condition. From here down, only the resolved
 1227: # values are used.
 1228: $MirrorPath = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($MirrorPath)
 1229: if ($OverrideOut) {
 1230:     $OverrideOut = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($OverrideOut)
 1231: }
 1232: 
 1233: # -EXTRAINPUT, PARSED BY HAND out of the remaining-argument catch-all.
 1234: # PowerShell's own binder refuses to bind one named parameter twice, even
 1235: # to an array, so a literal repeatable `-ExtraInput <path>` cannot be a
 1236: # declared parameter. Anything left in $BuildRemainingArgs that is not an
 1237: # "-ExtraInput <value>" pair is a parameter this tool does not have -
 1238: # this is also what refuses a re-mint or reseal flag: there is
 1239: # deliberately no such mode, so any flag claiming to be one lands here
 1240: # and is rejected the same way an unrecognized parameter always is.
 1241: $ExtraInputPaths = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
 1242: # Measured on both hosts: when NOTHING would otherwise land in a
 1243: # ValueFromRemainingArguments array, -File invocation still hands back
 1244: # one PHANTOM $null element instead of a genuinely empty array. Neither
 1245: # an -ExtraInput flag name nor a real value is ever $null or empty, so
 1246: # filtering both out removes exactly the phantom and nothing a real
 1247: # caller could have meant.
 1248: $remaining = @($BuildRemainingArgs | Where-Object { $_ })
 1249: $ri = 0
 1250: while ($ri -lt $remaining.Count) {
 1251:     $tok = [string]$remaining[$ri]
 1252:     if ($tok -cne "-ExtraInput") {
 1253:         Write-Output ("ERROR: unrecognized parameter '" + $tok + "'")
 1254:         exit 2
 1255:     }
 1256:     if ($ri + 1 -ge $remaining.Count) {
 1257:         Write-Output "ERROR: -ExtraInput requires a value"
 1258:         exit 2
 1259:     }
 1260:     $eiPath = [string]$remaining[$ri + 1]
 1261:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $eiPath -PathType Leaf)) {
 1262:         Write-Output ("ERROR: -ExtraInput '" + $eiPath + "' is not a file")
 1263:         exit 2
 1264:     }
 1265:     [void]$ExtraInputPaths.Add((Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $eiPath).Path)
 1266:     $ri += 2
 1267: }
 1268: 
 1269: # OVERLAP GUARD, before anything is created or deleted. -Force recursively
 1270: # deletes MirrorPath, so a MirrorPath equal to, inside, or containing
 1271: # RepoRoot would destroy the user's working tree. robocopy over an
 1272: # overlapping pair is equally unsafe. This runs FIRST, because by the time
 1273: # Remove-Item runs it is too late to check.
 1274: $rr = $RepoRoot.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
 1275: $mp = $MirrorPath.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
 1276: $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
 1277: if ($mp.Equals($rr, $cmp)) {
 1278:     Write-Output "ERROR: the mirror path is the repo root itself"
 1279:     exit 2
 1280: }
 1281: if ($mp.StartsWith($rr, $cmp)) {
 1282:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the mirror path is inside the repo ($MirrorPath)" +
 1283:         " - building or forcing there would write into, or delete, the" +
 1284:         " tree under review")
 1285:     exit 2
 1286: }
 1287: if ($rr.StartsWith($mp, $cmp)) {
 1288:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the mirror path contains the repo ($MirrorPath)" +
 1289:         " - -Force would delete the repo with it")
 1290:     exit 2
 1291: }
 1292: 
 1293: # Resolve the EFFECTIVE override path here, default included, and guard it
 1294: # beside the mirror guard. Deferring the default until after the mirror is
 1295: # built, copied, remediated and manifested would mean discovering a stale
 1296: # or overlapping artifact only after all that work had already happened,
 1297: # and -SkipProbe would bypass the check entirely.
 1298: if (-not $OverrideOut) {
 1299:     $OverrideOut = Join-Path (Split-Path $MirrorPath -Parent) `
 1300:         ((Split-Path $MirrorPath -Leaf) + ".skills-override.txt")
 1301: }
 1302: $op = $OverrideOut.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
 1303: foreach ($protected in @($rr, $mp)) {
 1304:     if (($op + "/").Equals($protected, $cmp) -or
 1305:         ($op + "/").StartsWith($protected, $cmp) -or
 1306:         $protected.StartsWith($op + "/", $cmp)) {
 1307:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the override path overlaps a protected tree" +
 1308:             " ($OverrideOut)")
 1309:         exit 2
 1310:     }
 1311: }
 1312: if (Test-Path $OverrideOut) {
 1313:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $OverrideOut already exists - a stale override" +
 1314:         " reads exactly like a fresh one")
 1315:     exit 2
 1316: }
 1317: 
 1318: # THE ADVISORY SOURCE MANIFEST'S DESTINATION, resolved and LEXICALLY
 1319: # guarded here, beside the override, for the same stated reason: a
 1320: # destination discovered after the build has copied, remediated and
 1321: # manifested is discovered too late, and -SkipProbe would bypass a check
 1322: # placed later. Its ALIAS guards are further down with the override's,
 1323: # and its removal is after both, because a build that is going to be
 1324: # refused must not have deleted anything first.
 1325: $smResult = Get-SourceManifestSidecarPath $MirrorPath
 1326: if ($smResult.Kind -eq "root") {
 1327:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the mirror path is a filesystem root" +
 1328:         " ($MirrorPath), which has no sibling, so no advisory source" +
 1329:         " manifest can be placed beside it")
 1330:     exit 2
 1331: }
 1332: if ($smResult.Kind -ne "ok") {
 1333:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the mirror path could not be resolved in order to" +
 1334:         " place an advisory source manifest beside it ($MirrorPath): " +
 1335:         $smResult.Reason)
 1336:     exit 2
 1337: }
 1338: $SourceManifestOut = $smResult.Path
 1339: $smp = $SourceManifestOut.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
 1340: foreach ($protected in @($rr, $mp)) {
 1341:     if (($smp + "/").Equals($protected, $cmp) -or
 1342:         ($smp + "/").StartsWith($protected, $cmp) -or
 1343:         $protected.StartsWith($smp + "/", $cmp)) {
 1344:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the source manifest path overlaps a protected" +
 1345:             " tree ($SourceManifestOut)")
 1346:         exit 2
 1347:     }
 1348: }
 1349: if ($smp.Equals($op, $cmp)) {
 1350:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the source manifest path is the override path" +
 1351:         " ($SourceManifestOut) - the advisory write would replace the file" +
 1352:         " the probe verified and the wrapper hashes")
 1353:     exit 2
 1354: }
 1355: if ($SourceManifestOut.Length -ge 260) {
 1356:     Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded by the source manifest - " +
 1357:         "$SourceManifestOut is $($SourceManifestOut.Length) characters " +
 1358:         "and the limit is 260")
 1359:     exit 2
 1360: }
 1361: 
 1362: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 1363: # PATH BUDGET PRE-FLIGHT. Runs here, after the overlap and override
 1364: # guards and BEFORE anything is created or deleted, because the failure
 1365: # it prevents is a MID-COPY one: robocopy stops at the first destination
 1366: # it cannot create, leaving a partially populated mirror that reads
 1367: # exactly like a complete one.
 1368: #
 1369: # THE UNIVERSE, frozen text: every file and directory destination that
 1370: # the exact `robocopy /E` operation may create beneath the resolved
 1371: # mirror root, including tracked, untracked, ignored, and all `.git`
 1372: # content. Two consequences that narrower readings get wrong - a
 1373: # directory with no files in it is still a destination, and `.git` is
 1374: # copied so `.git` counts.
 1375: #
 1376: # THE LIMIT is 260 characters as a conservative policy across both
 1377: # supported PowerShell hosts. It is a deterministic refusal threshold,
 1378: # not a claim about the maximum any host, API, OS configuration, or
 1379: # downstream client could support.
 1380: #
 1381: # THE ARITHMETIC is resolved mirror-root length, plus separator, plus
 1382: # the relative destination path length.
 1383: $PathBudget = 260
 1384: $budgetRoot = $MirrorPath.TrimEnd("\", "/")
 1385: $budgetRootLen = $budgetRoot.Length
 1386: 
 1387: # -OverrideOut is written BESIDE the mirror by this script, not by
 1388: # robocopy, so the copy universe never covers it. Its own check or none.
 1389: if ($OverrideOut.Length -ge $PathBudget) {
 1390:     Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded by the override file - " +
 1391:         "$OverrideOut is $($OverrideOut.Length) characters and the limit " +
 1392:         "is $PathBudget")
 1393:     exit 2
 1394: }
 1395: 
 1396: if ($budgetRootLen -ge $PathBudget) {
 1397:     Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded by the mirror root alone - " +
 1398:         "root is $budgetRootLen characters and the limit is $PathBudget")
 1399:     exit 2
 1400: }
 1401: 
 1402: $srcRoot = $RepoRoot.TrimEnd("\", "/")
 1403: # The prefix stripped to form a relative path, rebuilt rather than
 1404: # derived from a length. A drive root trims to "C:", and "C:".Length + 1
 1405: # is 3 only by coincidence of that one case; on any root ending in a
 1406: # colon the arithmetic silently under-measures every relative path,
 1407: # which is the permissive direction.
 1408: $srcPrefix = $srcRoot
 1409: if (-not $srcPrefix.EndsWith("\")) { $srcPrefix = $srcPrefix + "\" }
 1410: 
 1411: # The ROOT is a source path too. Checking only entries below it leaves
 1412: # the one reparse point most likely to exist unmeasured, and the
 1413: # contract clause says "a source reparse point" with no exemption.
 1414: try {
 1415:     $rootAttr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($srcRoot)
 1416: } catch {
 1417:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $srcRoot could not be enumerated, so the path " +
 1418:         "budget was never measured: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1419:     exit 2
 1420: }
 1421: if (($rootAttr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) {
 1422:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $srcRoot is a reparse point - the mirror refuses " +
 1423:         "to measure or copy across one")
 1424:     exit 2
 1425: }
 1426: 
 1427: $deepestLen = -1
 1428: $deepestRel = ""
 1429: $dirAttr = [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory
 1430: $reparseAttr = [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint
 1431: 
 1432: # Every directory link target this walk has already followed. Case
 1433: # insensitive because the filesystem is.
 1434: $followedTargets = New-Object "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" (
 1435:     [System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)
 1436: 
 1437: # Every directory link the walk meets that is NOT itself behind another
 1438: # link: its relative path under the source root and its resolved
 1439: # absolute target. The copy runs with /XJD and creates nothing beneath a
 1440: # link, so a link is ONE destination and the entries beneath it are not
 1441: # measured against the budget. The walk still DESCENDS through it,
 1442: # because the cycle refusal and the overlap refusal apply to every link
 1443: # the manifest can reach, and a link behind a link is reachable; a walk
 1444: # that stopped at the outer link never saw an inner cycle (cross-vendor
 1445: # round 1, 2026-09-05). A link behind a link is not recorded: it exists
 1446: # in the target and is reached through the outer junction.
 1447: $sourceLinks = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
 1448: 
 1449: $pending = New-Object System.Collections.Stack
 1450: $pending.Push(@{ Path = $srcRoot; UnderLink = $false })
 1451: while ($pending.Count -gt 0) {
 1452:     $item = $pending.Pop()
 1453:     $dir = $item.Path
 1454:     $underLink = $item.UnderLink
 1455:     $entries = $null
 1456:     try {
 1457:         $entries = @([System.IO.Directory]::GetFileSystemEntries($dir))
 1458:     } catch {
 1459:         # An unreadable path BLOCKS. It is never skipped: a path that
 1460:         # cannot be measured is not a path known to fit, and the same
 1461:         # hole semantics the manifest builder states apply here.
 1462:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $dir could not be enumerated, so the path " +
 1463:             "budget was never measured: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1464:         exit 2
 1465:     }
 1466:     foreach ($entry in $entries) {
 1467:         $attr = 0
 1468:         try {
 1469:             $attr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($entry)
 1470:         } catch {
 1471:             Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry could not be enumerated, so the " +
 1472:                 "path budget was never measured: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1473:             exit 2
 1474:         }
 1475:         # A source directory reparse point is NOT copied through: the
 1476:         # copy runs with /XJD, which excludes directory junctions and
 1477:         # directory symbolic links, so the link is one destination and
 1478:         # nothing beneath it is. The re-link step after the final sweep
 1479:         # re-creates it as a junction. The walk still enters it, for the
 1480:         # two refusals below only: measured 2026-09-05, the manifest
 1481:         # expansion starts a listing at every link it finds, so a link
 1482:         # onto one of its own ancestors or a tree whose links reach one
 1483:         # target twice must be refused wherever it sits.
 1484:         $isDirLink = $false
 1485:         if (($attr -band $reparseAttr) -ne 0 -and ($attr -band $dirAttr) -ne 0) {
 1486:             $isDirLink = $true
 1487:             # git's optional index refresh writes the repository's own
 1488:             # .git/index during every status capture. That is a write
 1489:             # into the repository and never through a link only while
 1490:             # .git is not itself a link, so one is refused here rather
 1491:             # than assumed away (cross-vendor round 2, 2026-09-05).
 1492:             if ([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($entry) -ieq ".git") {
 1493:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry - .git is a directory link, and" +
 1494:                     " the status captures write the repository's own index," +
 1495:                     " so this tree cannot be measured without writing" +
 1496:                     " through a link")
 1497:                 exit 2
 1498:             }
 1499:             $target = $null
 1500:             try {
 1501:                 $item = Get-Item -LiteralPath $entry -Force -ErrorAction Stop
 1502:                 $target = $item.Target
 1503:                 # Windows PowerShell 5.1 hands back a string COLLECTION here
 1504:                 # and PowerShell 7 hands back a plain string, so take the
 1505:                 # first element of anything that is not already a string
 1506:                 # rather than testing for one collection type.
 1507:                 if ($null -ne $target -and $target -isnot [string]) {
 1508:                     foreach ($candidate in $target) { $target = $candidate; break }
 1509:                 }
 1510:             } catch {
 1511:                 $target = $null
 1512:             }
 1513:             if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($target)) {
 1514:                 # An unresolvable link is the same class as an unreadable
 1515:                 # directory: a path that cannot be measured is not a path
 1516:                 # known to fit.
 1517:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry is a directory reparse point " +
 1518:                     "whose target could not be read, so the path budget was " +
 1519:                     "never measured")
 1520:                 exit 2
 1521:             }
 1522: 
 1523:             $entryFull = ""
 1524:             $targetFull = ""
 1525:             try {
 1526:                 $entryFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($entry)
 1527:                 # A RELATIVE TARGET IS RELATIVE TO THE LINK, NOT TO US.
 1528:                 # `GetFullPath` with one argument resolves against the
 1529:                 # PROCESS working directory, so a relative symbolic link
 1530:                 # target resolved to wherever this script happened to be
 1531:                 # invoked from: two distinct targets could compare equal
 1532:                 # and read as a repeat, and a genuine self-cycle could
 1533:                 # compare against the wrong absolute path and be missed.
 1534:                 # Junctions always store an absolute target, which is why
 1535:                 # the junction tests did not show it; symbolic links do
 1536:                 # not. Combine-then-normalize rather than the two-argument
 1537:                 # overload, which .NET Framework 4.8 does not carry.
 1538:                 if ([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($target)) {
 1539:                     $targetFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($target)
 1540:                 } else {
 1541:                     $linkParent = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($entryFull)
 1542:                     $targetFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath(
 1543:                         [System.IO.Path]::Combine($linkParent, $target))
 1544:                 }
 1545:             } catch {
 1546:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry is a directory reparse point " +
 1547:                     "whose target " + $target + " is not a usable path, so " +
 1548:                     "the path budget was never measured")
 1549:                 exit 2
 1550:             }
 1551:             $targetPrefix = $targetFull.TrimEnd("\") + "\"
 1552:             if ($entryFull.Equals($targetFull, "OrdinalIgnoreCase") -or
 1553:                 $entryFull.StartsWith($targetPrefix, "OrdinalIgnoreCase")) {
 1554:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry points at $targetFull, which " +
 1555:                     "contains it - following that link would never " +
 1556:                     "terminate, so the mirror refuses it")
 1557:                 exit 2
 1558:             }
 1559:             if (-not $followedTargets.Add($targetFull)) {
 1560:                 # Two links onto one target is not itself a cycle, but it is
 1561:                 # indistinguishable from one here without walking the whole
 1562:                 # graph, and refusing is the direction that cannot
 1563:                 # mismeasure.
 1564:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry points at $targetFull, which " +
 1565:                     "another link in this tree already reaches - the mirror " +
 1566:                     "refuses a tree whose links overlap")
 1567:                 exit 2
 1568:             }
 1569:             if (-not $underLink) {
 1570:                 [void]$sourceLinks.Add(@{ Rel = $entry.Substring($srcPrefix.Length)
 1571:                                           Target = $targetFull })
 1572:             }
 1573:         }
 1574:         # Only a destination counts. The link itself is one; nothing
 1575:         # beneath any link is created by the copy.
 1576:         if (-not $underLink) {
 1577:             $rel = $entry.Substring($srcPrefix.Length)
 1578:             if ($rel.Length -gt $deepestLen) {
 1579:                 $deepestLen = $rel.Length
 1580:                 $deepestRel = $rel
 1581:             }
 1582:         }
 1583:         if (($attr -band $dirAttr) -ne 0) {
 1584:             $pending.Push(@{ Path = $entry; UnderLink = ($underLink -or $isDirLink) })
 1585:         }
 1586:     }
 1587: }
 1588: 
 1589: if ($deepestLen -ge 0) {
 1590:     $deepestSum = $budgetRootLen + 1 + $deepestLen
 1591:     if ($deepestSum -ge $PathBudget) {
 1592:         Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded - mirror root is " +
 1593:             "$budgetRootLen characters, the deepest relative destination " +
 1594:             "is $deepestLen characters, their sum is $deepestSum, and the " +
 1595:             "limit is $PathBudget. Build the mirror at a shorter path. " +
 1596:             "Deepest: $deepestRel")
 1597:         exit 2
 1598:     }
 1599: }
 1600: 
 1601: # LINK TARGETS ARE PROTECTED TREES. The overlap guard above compares the
 1602: # mirror and override paths against the source root only, and a link
 1603: # target lives outside it. Cross-vendor round 1 (2026-09-05) named the
 1604: # case: -MirrorPath at a link's target with -Force would delete the
 1605: # user's reference checkout, and -OverrideOut inside it would write
 1606: # there. Round 2 added two more: an INNER link's target is reached by
 1607: # the manifest just the same, so every target the walk followed is
 1608: # protected, not only the recorded outer links; and a path spelled
 1609: # THROUGH some other link can land inside a protected target without
 1610: # matching its spelling, so a mirror or override path with a reparse
 1611: # point among its existing ancestors is refused outright rather than
 1612: # compared. This runs before anything is created or deleted.
 1613: function Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink($path) {
 1614:     # The path itself, then each existing ancestor up to the drive root:
 1615:     # is any of them a reparse point? Attributes are read directly
 1616:     # rather than after a Test-Path, because a DANGLING junction is a
 1617:     # reparse point Test-Path may report as absent; a missing entry is
 1618:     # the one condition that skips a level, and it is recognised by the
 1619:     # exception type, never by a false from a helper. Returns the
 1620:     # offending path or $null.
 1621:     $probe = ([string]$path).TrimEnd("\", "/")
 1622:     while ($probe -and -not [string]::IsNullOrEmpty([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($probe))) {
 1623:         $pa = 0
 1624:         $missing = $false
 1625:         try {
 1626:             $pa = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($probe)
 1627:         } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
 1628:             $missing = $true
 1629:         } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
 1630:             $missing = $true
 1631:         } catch {
 1632:             Write-Output ("ERROR: " + $probe + " could not be read while" +
 1633:                 " checking for a directory link: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1634:             exit 2
 1635:         }
 1636:         if (-not $missing -and (($pa -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0)) {
 1637:             return $probe
 1638:         }
 1639:         $probe = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($probe)
 1640:     }
 1641:     return $null
 1642: }
 1643: 
 1644: foreach ($pair in @(@("mirror path", $MirrorPath), @("override path", $OverrideOut),
 1645:                     @("source manifest path", $SourceManifestOut))) {
 1646:     $label = $pair[0]
 1647:     $hit = Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink $pair[1]
 1648:     if ($hit) {
 1649:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the " + $label + " passes through a directory" +
 1650:             " link at " + $hit + " - a path reached through a link can alias" +
 1651:             " a tree the mirror only links to, so the mirror refuses it")
 1652:         exit 2
 1653:     }
 1654: }
 1655: # A followed TARGET that is itself a link, or sits beneath one, is the
 1656: # alias in the other direction (cross-vendor round 3): the walk records
 1657: # the target as spelled, so a mirror path at the real directory behind
 1658: # it neither passes through a link nor overlaps the recorded text.
 1659: foreach ($target in @($followedTargets)) {
 1660:     $hit = Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink $target
 1661:     if ($hit) {
 1662:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the source link target " + $target + " is" +
 1663:             " itself a directory link or sits beneath one (" + $hit + ") -" +
 1664:             " the mirror cannot protect a tree it cannot name, so it" +
 1665:             " refuses to build")
 1666:         exit 2
 1667:     }
 1668: }
 1669: foreach ($target in @($followedTargets)) {
 1670:     $tp = ([string]$target).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
 1671:     foreach ($pair in @(@("mirror path", $mp), @("override path", ($op + "/")),
 1672:                         @("source manifest path", ($smp + "/")))) {
 1673:         $label = $pair[0]
 1674:         $cand = $pair[1]
 1675:         if ($cand.Equals($tp, $cmp) -or $cand.StartsWith($tp, $cmp) -or
 1676:             $tp.StartsWith($cand, $cmp)) {
 1677:             Write-Output ("ERROR: the " + $label + " overlaps the target of a" +
 1678:                 " source link (" + $target + ") - building there would write" +
 1679:                 " into, or delete, a tree the mirror only links to")
 1680:             exit 2
 1681:         }
 1682:     }
 1683: }
 1684: 
 1685: # EXTRA INPUTS versus the sidecar. $ExtraInputPaths holds paths already
 1686: # resolved by the -ExtraInput parser above; that parser runs before $smp
 1687: # exists, which is why this check lives here rather than beside it.
 1688: foreach ($eiResolved in @($ExtraInputPaths)) {
 1689:     $eiNorm = ([string]$eiResolved).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
 1690:     if ($eiNorm.Equals($smp, $cmp)) {
 1691:         Write-Output ("ERROR: -ExtraInput '" + $eiResolved + "' is the" +
 1692:             " source manifest path, which this build replaces - a declared" +
 1693:             " review input must not be a file this tool overwrites")
 1694:         exit 2
 1695:     }
 1696:     # SPELLING EQUALITY IS NOT IDENTITY, and the comparison above is only
 1697:     # spelling. With C:\alias a junction to C:\out, a mirror at
 1698:     # C:\out\mirror and an extra input at
 1699:     # C:\alias\mirror.source-manifest, BOTH output paths have ordinary
 1700:     # ancestors and pass their own alias checks, while the extra input
 1701:     # keeps the alias spelling and compares unequal - and -Force would
 1702:     # then remove the very file that extra input names, after which the
 1703:     # unchecked Copy-Item can leave it out of the mirror entirely. This
 1704:     # tool cannot establish physical identity here, so it REFUSES the
 1705:     # case it cannot decide rather than guessing.
 1706:     # A TRAILING DOT OR SPACE ON ANY COMPONENT defeats the comparison
 1707:     # above. Windows strips it when it OPENS the file; PowerShell keeps
 1708:     # it in the path. Measured 2026-09-05: `BACKLOG.md.` passes
 1709:     # Test-Path, resolves WITH the dot, hashes identical to
 1710:     # `BACKLOG.md`, and compares unequal - so an extra input spelled
 1711:     # `<mirror>.source-manifest.` passes this guard and still names the
 1712:     # file -Force is about to remove. Refuse the spelling.
 1713:     foreach ($seg in $eiNorm.Split('/')) {
 1714:         if ($seg -match '[. ]$') {
 1715:             Write-Output ("ERROR: -ExtraInput '" + $eiResolved + "' has" +
 1716:                 " a path component ending in a dot or a space, which" +
 1717:                 " Windows strips when it opens the file but PowerShell" +
 1718:                 " keeps in the path, so this tool cannot tell which file" +
 1719:                 " it names; pass the exact name")
 1720:             exit 2
 1721:         }
 1722:     }
 1723:     $eiHit = Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink $eiResolved
 1724:     if ($eiHit) {
 1725:         Write-Output ("ERROR: -ExtraInput '" + $eiResolved + "' is reached" +
 1726:             " through a directory link at " + $eiHit + ", so this tool" +
 1727:             " cannot tell whether it names the same file as the source" +
 1728:             " manifest it replaces; pass the path behind the link")
 1729:         exit 2
 1730:     }
 1731: }
 1732: 
 1733: # LAST, because a build that is going to be refused must not have deleted
 1734: # anything first. Read the ATTRIBUTES rather than calling Test-Path,
 1735: # because the Directory bit and the not-there case have to be told apart
 1736: # in one read, and this is the one place where a wrong "it is not there"
 1737: # turns into a write.
 1738: #
 1739: # MEASURED 2026-09-05 under BOTH hosts, because the link walker above
 1740: # justifies the same choice with a claim nobody had measured. Windows
 1741: # PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7 agree, character for character, on all
 1742: # four cases: an intact junction, a DANGLING junction, a plainly missing
 1743: # path, and an ordinary file. The dangling junction returns
 1744: # `Directory, ReparsePoint` from GetAttributes and `True` from Test-Path
 1745: # on both, and only the missing path throws. So the cross-host risk this
 1746: # step was escalated for does not exist for a junction. Two limits are
 1747: # stated rather than papered over: the walker's premise that Test-Path
 1748: # "may report as absent" was NOT reproduced for a dangling junction, and
 1749: # a dangling FILE SYMLINK was not measured at all, so nothing here claims
 1750: # anything about one.
 1751: $smAttr = $null
 1752: try {
 1753:     $smAttr = [System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($SourceManifestOut)
 1754: } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
 1755:     $smAttr = $null
 1756: } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
 1757:     $smAttr = $null
 1758: } catch {
 1759:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the source manifest path could not be examined" +
 1760:         " ($SourceManifestOut): " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1761:     exit 2
 1762: }
 1763: if ($null -ne $smAttr) {
 1764:     if (([int]$smAttr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -ne 0) {
 1765:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $SourceManifestOut is a directory - this tool" +
 1766:             " replaces a file there and never removes a tree")
 1767:         exit 2
 1768:     }
 1769:     if (-not $Force) {
 1770:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $SourceManifestOut already exists - pass" +
 1771:             " -Force to replace it, the same rule the mirror path follows")
 1772:         exit 2
 1773:     }
 1774:     # REMOVING a link removes the link and never its target's bytes,
 1775:     # which is exactly why the removal is safe where a write through it
 1776:     # was not.
 1777:     Remove-Item -LiteralPath $SourceManifestOut -Force
 1778: }
 1779: 
 1780: if (Test-Path $MirrorPath) {
 1781:     if (-not $Force) {
 1782:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $MirrorPath already exists - a stale mirror" +
 1783:             " reads exactly like a fresh one. Pass -Force to replace it.")
 1784:         exit 2
 1785:     }
 1786:     Remove-Item -LiteralPath $MirrorPath -Recurse -Force
 1787: }
 1788: New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $MirrorPath | Out-Null
 1789: $MirrorPath = (Resolve-Path $MirrorPath).Path
 1790: 
 1791: # CONSTRUCTION BRIDGE, step 1: capture the source identity BEFORE the
 1792: # copy. Everything after this compares against this value, not against a
 1793: # fresh read, because a fresh read cannot tell a stable source from one
 1794: # that moved and moved back.
 1795: $sourceBefore = Get-HeadSha $RepoRoot
 1796: if (-not $sourceBefore.Ok) {
 1797:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $sourceBefore.Reason + " - the mirror's" +
 1798:         " identity in the debate record would be blank on the source side")
 1799:     exit 1
 1800: }
 1801: 
 1802: # The source status, captured BEFORE the copy. Taken here and again
 1803: # after, and required equal, for the same reason as the head: a
 1804: # fingerprint taken only afterwards bakes in any edit that landed during
 1805: # the copy, and every later verify then passes over a mirror carrying
 1806: # older bytes. That direction is fail-OPEN, which this gate may not be.
 1807: $statusBefore = Get-StatusSha256 $RepoRoot
 1808: if (-not $statusBefore.Ok) {
 1809:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status capture failed (" +
 1810:         $statusBefore.Reason + "), so post-construction drift in an" +
 1811:         " untracked or ignored review input would be undetectable")
 1812:     exit 1
 1813: }
 1814: 
 1815: # /XJD: directory junctions and directory symbolic links are not copied
 1816: # through. Measured 2026-09-05: the link is absent from the destination
 1817: # and every ordinary file copies. File links are NOT excluded, so a file
 1818: # symbolic link still lands as its target's bytes, exactly as before.
 1819: & robocopy $RepoRoot $MirrorPath /E /XJD /COPY:DAT /R:1 /W:1 /NFL /NDL /NJH /NJS /NP | Out-Null
 1820: if ($LASTEXITCODE -ge 8) {
 1821:     Write-Output "ERROR: robocopy failed with $LASTEXITCODE"
 1822:     exit 2
 1823: }
 1824: 
 1825: # THE THREE TEST SEAMS, and the rule they must satisfy LITERALLY. Two of
 1826: # them are declared here; the third, $seamFailLinkTarget, is declared at
 1827: # the top of the execution section because Get-FilesBeneath reads it
 1828: # during the source status capture ABOVE, and it only ever ADDS the
 1829: # link-target resolution failure inside that expansion.
 1830: #
 1831: # Each is a BOOLEAN that ORs one extra block condition into a comparison
 1832: # below. None can supply a value, so none can suppress a real
 1833: # measurement, and setting one can only ever ADD a reason to fail.
 1834: # That makes the one-way property structural rather than a property of
 1835: # whatever value someone happens to pass.
 1836: #
 1837: # TWO earlier shapes failed that bar, and both were caught by review
 1838: # rather than by reasoning, which is why the property is now asserted by
 1839: # a test instead of argued in a comment:
 1840: #
 1841: #   1. A copy-source override aimed at a same-HEAD tree with different
 1842: #      ignored content would have BUILT, carrying the wrong non-HEAD
 1843: #      content under a record attesting the real source. Its partner
 1844: #      committed into $RepoRoot, contradicting this tool's promise three
 1845: #      lines from the top that it never writes to the real tree.
 1846: #   2. The replacement INJECTED a value. A parent that set it to the
 1847: #      repo's own current HEAD - trivially readable in advance -
 1848: #      suppressed the genuine post-copy measurement and turned a build
 1849: #      that should have failed into one that passed. That is the exact
 1850: #      inverse of the claim the comment was making.
 1851: #
 1852: # Any parent process can set these variables, so the shape has to be
 1853: # safe on its own, not safe because no shipped caller sets it.
 1854: $seamFailSourceStable = [bool]$env:PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_SOURCE_STABLE
 1855: $seamFailCopiedHead = [bool]$env:PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_COPIED_HEAD
 1856: 
 1857: $sourceAfter = Get-HeadSha $RepoRoot
 1858: 
 1859: # BRIDGE STEP 3: the source must not have moved while we copied it. A
 1860: # source that moved mid-copy produces a tree that matches no commit.
 1861: if (-not $sourceAfter.Ok) {
 1862:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $sourceAfter.Reason + " after the copy")
 1863:     exit 1
 1864: }
 1865: if ($seamFailSourceStable -or ($sourceAfter.Sha -ne $sourceBefore.Sha)) {
 1866:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source head moved during construction (" +
 1867:         $sourceBefore.Sha + " to " + $sourceAfter.Sha + ") - the copied tree" +
 1868:         " matches neither commit")
 1869:     exit 1
 1870: }
 1871: 
 1872: # BRIDGE STEP 4: the COPIED tree must carry the source's head. Without
 1873: # this the record can hold two individually valid SHAs while nothing
 1874: # proves the mirror was built from the recorded source commit - two true
 1875: # facts arranged to look like one.
 1876: $copiedHead = Get-HeadSha $MirrorPath
 1877: if (-not $copiedHead.Ok) {
 1878:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $copiedHead.Reason + " - the copied tree's" +
 1879:         " identity could not be measured")
 1880:     exit 1
 1881: }
 1882: if ($seamFailCopiedHead -or ($copiedHead.Sha -ne $sourceBefore.Sha)) {
 1883:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror was not built from this source (source" +
 1884:         " head " + $sourceBefore.Sha + ", copied head " + $copiedHead.Sha + ")")
 1885:     exit 1
 1886: }
 1887: 
 1888: # The second half of the status bridge: re-capture and require equal, so
 1889: # an edit that landed during the copy fails the build instead of being
 1890: # baked into the record. Never re-derived from the mirror, which is about
 1891: # to be remediated and would then disagree by design.
 1892: $sourceStatus = Get-StatusSha256 $RepoRoot
 1893: if (-not $sourceStatus.Ok) {
 1894:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status capture failed (" +
 1895:         $sourceStatus.Reason + "), so post-construction drift in an" +
 1896:         " untracked or ignored review input would be undetectable")
 1897:     exit 1
 1898: }
 1899: if ($sourceStatus.Sha -ne $statusBefore.Sha) {
 1900:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: a source review input changed during" +
 1901:         " construction, so the mirror carries neither the before nor the" +
 1902:         " after state of the tree")
 1903:     exit 1
 1904: }
 1905: 
 1906: $found = Get-BackChannelEntry $MirrorPath
 1907: if (-not $found.Ok) {
 1908:     Write-Output ("ERROR: could not enumerate back-channels in the" +
 1909:         " mirror: " + $found.Reason)
 1910:     exit 2
 1911: }
 1912: $entries = $found.Entries
 1913: $tracked = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
 1914: foreach ($entry in $entries) {
 1915:     if (Test-Tracked $MirrorPath $entry) { [void]$tracked.Add($entry) }
 1916:     $full = Join-Path $MirrorPath $entry
 1917:     if (Test-Path $full) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $full -Recurse -Force }
 1918:     # Prune the directories the entry lived in, up to but never including
 1919:     # the mirror root. `git ls-files` names FILES, so deleting one leaves
 1920:     # `.agents/skills/<name>/` standing - an empty shell of the very
 1921:     # surface this step exists to remove.
 1922:     $dir = Split-Path (Join-Path $MirrorPath $entry) -Parent
 1923:     while ($dir -and ($dir.Length -gt $MirrorPath.Length) -and
 1924:            (Test-Path $dir) -and
 1925:            -not (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $dir -Force)) {
 1926:         Remove-Item -LiteralPath $dir -Force
 1927:         $dir = Split-Path $dir -Parent
 1928:     }
 1929: }
 1930: if ($tracked.Count -gt 0) {
 1931:     # Suppress repository hooks for the remediation commit BELOW. The
 1932:     # mirror carries the real repo's .git, hooks included (see the
 1933:     # BLOCKED text on the commit call), so committing here without
 1934:     # redirecting core.hooksPath would run whatever hook the reviewed
 1935:     # repo ships. The directory lives inside the MIRROR's own .git,
 1936:     # where a plain filesystem copy carries it along for free but
 1937:     # `git status`/`ls-files` never see it, so it cannot register as a
 1938:     # back-channel or dirty the porcelain itself.
 1939:     #
 1940:     # Created fresh and VERIFIED empty in this same run, then used for
 1941:     # BOTH git calls below. Never fall back to committing with hooks
 1942:     # live: either check failing exits BLOCKED before either call runs.
 1943:     $hooksDir = Join-Path $MirrorPath (Join-Path ".git" "parallax-mirror-hooks")
 1944:     try {
 1945:         New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $hooksDir -Force -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null
 1946:     } catch {
 1947:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: could not create an empty hooks directory" +
 1948:             " for the remediation commit (" + $_.Exception.Message + ")." +
 1949:             " Never fall back to committing with hooks live.")
 1950:         exit 1
 1951:     }
 1952:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $hooksDir -PathType Container)) {
 1953:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the hooks directory for the remediation" +
 1954:             " commit is not a directory. Never fall back to committing" +
 1955:             " with hooks live.")
 1956:         exit 1
 1957:     }
 1958:     $hooksDirEntries = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $hooksDir -Force)
 1959:     if ($hooksDirEntries.Count -gt 0) {
 1960:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the hooks directory for the remediation" +
 1961:             " commit is not empty (" + $hooksDirEntries.Count +
 1962:             " entries). Never fall back to committing with hooks live.")
 1963:         exit 1
 1964:     }
 1965:     $hooksOverride = "core.hooksPath=" + $hooksDir
 1966: 
 1967:     # Stage exactly the entries that were tracked. `git add -A` over a
 1968:     # pathspec would fail when one of the two patterns matches nothing,
 1969:     # and `git add -A` over the whole tree would stage the untracked and
 1970:     # ignored files the mirror deliberately carries.
 1971:     $paths = @($tracked)
 1972:     & git -C $MirrorPath -c $hooksOverride add -- @paths 2>&1 | Out-Null
 1973:     if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
 1974:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: could not stage the removed back-channels" +
 1975:             " in the mirror")
 1976:         exit 1
 1977:     }
 1978:     & git -C $MirrorPath -c $hooksOverride -c user.email=parallax@local `
 1979:         -c user.name=parallax commit -q -m "remove instruction back-channels for review" 2>&1 | Out-Null
 1980:     if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
 1981:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror commit failed. The mirror carries" +
 1982:             " the real repo's .git, hooks included, so this is a" +
 1983:             " mirror-construction problem, never a finding about the" +
 1984:             " reviewed work.")
 1985:         exit 1
 1986:     }
 1987: }
 1988: 
 1989: $after = Get-BackChannelEntry $MirrorPath
 1990: if (-not $after.Ok) {
 1991:     Write-Output ("ERROR: could not re-enumerate back-channels in the" +
 1992:         " mirror: " + $after.Reason)
 1993:     exit 2
 1994: }
 1995: if ($after.Entries.Count -gt 0) {
 1996:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: back-channel(s) survived remediation: " +
 1997:         ($after.Entries -join "; "))
 1998:     exit 1
 1999: }
 2000: 
 2001: # COPY EACH -ExtraInput IN NOW, as part of construction and BEFORE the
 2002: # baseline and the manifest are taken below, so the identity record
 2003: # covers it and the printed record enumerates it - it lands as an
 2004: # ordinary untracked mirror file, the same as any other review input the
 2005: # mirror cannot inherit on its own.
 2006: # Two refusals BEFORE any of them is copied, so a rejected build never
 2007: # leaves half the inputs in place. Every input is flattened to its leaf
 2008: # name, because the mirror re-roots every path; that makes two different
 2009: # files with one leaf name indistinguishable at the destination, and it
 2010: # lets an outside file land on a name the copied tree already carries.
 2011: # Cross-vendor round 2 found both: the loop wrote with -Force, so the
 2012: # second of a colliding pair silently won and a tracked file could be
 2013: # replaced by unreviewed content that the digest then certified. The
 2014: # plan promises to copy in EACH named file, and there is no second
 2015: # location it ever froze, so the honest answer is to refuse.
 2016: $eiSeen = @{}
 2017: foreach ($ei in @($ExtraInputPaths)) {
 2018:     $leaf = Split-Path $ei -Leaf
 2019:     $key = $leaf.ToLowerInvariant()
 2020:     if ($eiSeen.ContainsKey($key)) {
 2021:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: two -ExtraInput files share the destination" +
 2022:             " name '" + $leaf + "' (" + $eiSeen[$key] + " and " + $ei +
 2023:             "); the mirror flattens every input to its leaf name, so one" +
 2024:             " would silently replace the other")
 2025:         exit 1
 2026:     }
 2027:     $eiSeen[$key] = $ei
 2028:     $dest = Join-Path $MirrorPath $leaf
 2029:     if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dest) {
 2030:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: -ExtraInput '" + $leaf + "' would overwrite" +
 2031:             " a file the mirror already has (" + $dest + "); reviewed" +
 2032:             " content must not be replaced by unreviewed content the" +
 2033:             " digest would then certify")
 2034:         exit 1
 2035:     }
 2036: }
 2037: foreach ($ei in @($ExtraInputPaths)) {
 2038:     $dest = Join-Path $MirrorPath (Split-Path $ei -Leaf)
 2039:     Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ei -Destination $dest -Force
 2040: }
 2041: # THE LAST SWEEP, after every writer into the mirror. The enumeration
 2042: # above proves REMEDIATION worked. This one proves nothing put a
 2043: # back-channel back afterwards. SKILL.md calls the enumeration the
 2044: # PRIMARY control for the reviewed tree's skills and agents, "not
 2045: # defence in depth", and a control that runs before the last writer
 2046: # into the tree it clears is not that control. -ExtraInput is the only
 2047: # shipped writer below the sweep above; this sweep sits after ALL of
 2048: # them so the next writer added inherits it rather than slipping under
 2049: # it. Measured 2026-08-31: without this, an AGENTS.md named as an
 2050: # -ExtraInput reached the finished mirror and only the client probe
 2051: # refused it.
 2052: $final = Get-BackChannelEntry $MirrorPath
 2053: if (-not $final.Ok) {
 2054:     Write-Output ("ERROR: could not re-enumerate back-channels in the" +
 2055:         " finished mirror: " + $final.Reason)
 2056:     exit 2
 2057: }
 2058: if ($final.Entries.Count -gt 0) {
 2059:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: back-channel(s) present in the finished" +
 2060:         " mirror: " + ($final.Entries -join "; ") + ". Remediation" +
 2061:         " cleared the mirror, so this was carried back in afterwards -" +
 2062:         " an -ExtraInput may not name an instruction channel.")
 2063:     exit 1
 2064: }
 2065: 
 2066: # RE-LINK, after the last writer and the last sweep. Remediation deletes
 2067: # what it finds with Remove-Item, and after this step that path would
 2068: # reach THROUGH a junction into the user's real reference checkout. So
 2069: # the junctions are created here, once nothing above can delete again,
 2070: # and the check that follows is READ-ONLY.
 2071: #
 2072: # Always a JUNCTION, whatever the source had. Measured 2026-09-05: a
 2073: # symbolic link needs a privilege this tool cannot assume and mklink /D
 2074: # refused it; New-Item -ItemType Junction succeeded on both hosts with
 2075: # none. The reviewer reads a junction as a directory.
 2076: foreach ($lnk in @($sourceLinks)) {
 2077:     $dest = Join-Path $MirrorPath $lnk.Rel
 2078:     if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dest) {
 2079:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the copy created '" + $lnk.Rel + "' although" +
 2080:             " /XJD excludes directory links, so the mirror already holds" +
 2081:             " something at a path that must be a junction")
 2082:         exit 1
 2083:     }
 2084:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $lnk.Target -PathType Container)) {
 2085:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the link target " + $lnk.Target + " for '" +
 2086:             $lnk.Rel + "' could not be read at re-link time, so the mirror" +
 2087:             " would be missing a review input that reads as complete")
 2088:         exit 2
 2089:     }
 2090:     $parent = Split-Path $dest -Parent
 2091:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $parent -PathType Container)) {
 2092:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the parent directory of '" + $lnk.Rel + "'" +
 2093:             " is missing from the mirror, so the copy did not produce the" +
 2094:             " tree the walk measured")
 2095:         exit 1
 2096:     }
 2097:     try {
 2098:         New-Item -ItemType Junction -Path $dest -Target $lnk.Target `
 2099:             -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null
 2100:     } catch {
 2101:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: could not re-create '" + $lnk.Rel + "' as a" +
 2102:             " junction onto " + $lnk.Target + ": " + $_.Exception.Message)
 2103:         exit 1
 2104:     }
 2105:     # Read the junction back. A link that does not resolve to the
 2106:     # recorded target is a mirror pointing somewhere the record does not
 2107:     # say, which is the case the whole identity gate exists to refuse.
 2108:     $made = $null
 2109:     try {
 2110:         $made = (Get-Item -LiteralPath $dest -Force -ErrorAction Stop).Target
 2111:         if ($null -ne $made -and $made -isnot [string]) {
 2112:             foreach ($candidate in $made) { $made = $candidate; break }
 2113:         }
 2114:     } catch { $made = $null }
 2115:     if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($made)) {
 2116:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the junction at '" + $lnk.Rel + "' was" +
 2117:             " created but its target could not be read back")
 2118:         exit 1
 2119:     }
 2120:     $madeFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($made).TrimEnd("\")
 2121:     if (-not $madeFull.Equals($lnk.Target.TrimEnd("\"), "OrdinalIgnoreCase")) {
 2122:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the junction at '" + $lnk.Rel + "' resolves" +
 2123:             " to " + $madeFull + " rather than the recorded " + $lnk.Target)
 2124:         exit 1
 2125:     }
 2126: }
 2127: 
 2128: # THE LINK SWEEP, read-only by construction. git walks a junction onto a
 2129: # plain folder file by file, so a back-channel behind a link is now
 2130: # enumerable, and deleting it would delete from the user's real tree.
 2131: # Any such entry is a BLOCK with its path named; nothing here removes
 2132: # anything. A link onto a directory holding its own .git is ONE entry to
 2133: # git and is not descended; that blind spot is backlog item 92, and it
 2134: # is the same blind spot the copy had.
 2135: if (@($sourceLinks).Count -gt 0) {
 2136:     $linkSweep = Get-BackChannelEntry $MirrorPath
 2137:     if (-not $linkSweep.Ok) {
 2138:         Write-Output ("ERROR: could not enumerate back-channels behind the" +
 2139:             " re-linked paths: " + $linkSweep.Reason)
 2140:         exit 2
 2141:     }
 2142:     $behind = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
 2143:     foreach ($e in @($linkSweep.Entries)) {
 2144:         $eNorm = ([string]$e).Replace("\", "/")
 2145:         foreach ($lnk in @($sourceLinks)) {
 2146:             $prefix = ([string]$lnk.Rel).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
 2147:             if ($eNorm.StartsWith($prefix, "OrdinalIgnoreCase")) {
 2148:                 [void]$behind.Add($eNorm)
 2149:                 break
 2150:             }
 2151:         }
 2152:     }
 2153:     if ($behind.Count -gt 0) {
 2154:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: back-channel(s) under a re-linked path: " +
 2155:             ($behind -join "; ") + ". The build never deletes through a" +
 2156:             " link; remove the file from the link's target and rebuild.")
 2157:         exit 1
 2158:     }
 2159: }
 2160: 
 2161: $head = (& git -C $MirrorPath rev-parse HEAD 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
 2162: if (($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) -or -not $head) {
 2163:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: could not resolve the mirror's HEAD - the" +
 2164:         " mirror's identity in the debate record would be blank")
 2165:     exit 1
 2166: }
 2167: $captured = Get-BaselineRaw $MirrorPath
 2168: if (-not $captured.Ok) {
 2169:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the baseline capture failed (" +
 2170:         $captured.Reason + "). A failed capture printed as success would" +
 2171:         " quarantine every round of the review that follows, or absorb" +
 2172:         " changes it should have caught.")
 2173:     exit 1
 2174: }
 2175: $parsed = ConvertTo-StatusRecord $captured.Fields
 2176: if ($parsed.ContainsKey("Error")) {
 2177:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $parsed.Error)
 2178:     exit 1
 2179: }
 2180: $baseline = @(@($parsed.Records) |
 2181:     ForEach-Object { Format-StatusRecord $_ })
 2182: $subjects = Get-ManifestSubject $parsed.Records
 2183: if ($subjects.ContainsKey("Error")) {
 2184:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $subjects.Error)
 2185:     exit 1
 2186: }
 2187: $manifestResult = Get-ContentManifest $MirrorPath $subjects.Paths
 2188: if ($manifestResult.ContainsKey("Error")) {
 2189:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $manifestResult.Error)
 2190:     exit 1
 2191: }
 2192: $manifest = $manifestResult.Paths
 2193: 
 2194: # ONE digest over the baseline just captured and the manifest just
 2195: # built, so it describes exactly the tree those two printed sections
 2196: # describe - not a second, separately-timed measurement of the mirror
 2197: # that could disagree with what was printed above it.
 2198: $mirrorStateSha256 = Get-CombinedSha256 $captured.Fields $manifest
 2199: 
 2200: $probeLine = "skipped"
 2201: $overrideFile = ""
 2202: if (-not $SkipProbe) {
 2203:     # $OverrideOut was resolved and guarded at the top. The probe's
 2204:     # verified value IS the dispatch's input, so a mirror built without it
 2205:     # leaves the transport with a file that does not exist.
 2206:     $probeScript = Join-Path (Split-Path $PSCommandPath -Parent) "codex-context-probe.ps1"
 2207:     $hostExe = (Get-Process -Id $PID).Path
 2208:     $probeOut = & $hostExe -NoProfile -NonInteractive -File $probeScript `
 2209:         -WorkDir $MirrorPath -SuppressSkills -OverrideOut $OverrideOut `
 2210:         -Json -CodexCommand $CodexCommand
 2211:     if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
 2212:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the client context probe did not pass: " +
 2213:             (@($probeOut) -join "`n"))
 2214:         exit 1
 2215:     }
 2216:     $probeLine = (@($probeOut) -join "`n").Trim()
 2217:     $overrideFile = $OverrideOut
 2218: }
 2219: 
 2220: # The advisory source manifest, written before the record so the record
 2221: # can name it. Its destination was fully guarded above; a failure to
 2222: # write it here is reported and never fatal.
 2223: $sidecarRecord = $SourceManifestOut
 2224: if (-not (Write-SourceManifestSidecar $SourceManifestOut $sourceStatus.Manifest)) {
 2225:     $sidecarRecord = "unwritable"
 2226: }
 2227: 
 2228: Write-Output ("mirror: " + $MirrorPath)
 2229: # TWO identities, not one. They differ whenever remediation committed,
 2230: # which is the ordinary case for a repo carrying a tracked back-channel,
 2231: # so a record printing one of them twice would be wrong in the common
 2232: # case rather than the rare one.
 2233: Write-Output ("source_head: " + $sourceBefore.Sha)
 2234: Write-Output ("mirror_head: " + $head)
 2235: Write-Output ("source_status_sha256: " + $sourceStatus.Sha)
 2236: Write-Output ("mirror_state_sha256: " + $mirrorStateSha256)
 2237: Write-Output "baseline:"
 2238: foreach ($b in $baseline) { Write-Output ("  " + $b) }
 2239: Write-Output "manifest:"
 2240: foreach ($m in $manifest) { Write-Output ("  " + $m) }
 2241: Write-Output "links:"
